String types `WageFluctuationKind` (`"rise"/"fall"`) and `WageOutcomeDirection` (`"rises"/"falls"/"unchanged"`); value objects `WageFluctuation` + `ComputeWageFluctuation`; `SphereWageOutcome` (centi-unit old/new/change prices, composition, direction); `WageEffectAnalysisID` + `NewWageEffectAnalysisID()` + `IsZero()`; `WageEffectAnalysis` struct + `Validate()`; `SphereInput` helper. Package-private helpers: `sphereWagePrice` (truncating integer division: `k_centi*(10000+rate)/10000`), `wageAdjustedGeneralRate` (round-half-up), `baseGeneralRate` (round-half-up). Public functions: `ComputeSphereWageOutcome`, `ComputeWageEffectAnalysis`.
- **Store.** `Store` interface gains `Create/Get/List WageEffectAnalysis`. `memory.go` adds one map. `mysql.go` adds INSERT/SELECT/LIST methods for `wage_effect_analyses`; `AverageOutcome` and `Outcomes` are JSON columns (mirrors `GeneralProfitRate.Spheres` pattern). Migrations `00021_v3_ch11_wage_effect.sql` (table `wage_effect_analyses`) and `00022_v3_ch11_seed.sql` (§main analysis: base 80c+20v s′=100% factor=125; seed ID `5eed…1101`).
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/avgprofit/wage-effect` (400 bad json; 422 negative magnitude; 201+Location); `GET /v1/avgprofit/wage-effect` (list, `{"items":[...]}`); `GET /v1/avgprofit/wage-effect/{id}` (404 missing). All proxied through api-gateway.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch11WageFluctuations.tsx` (registered `v3-ch11`): wage-change % slider (50–150); base composition inputs; general rate before/after with direction arrow; table of average/lower/higher spheres showing old vs new price of production (÷100 for display) with rise/fall/unchanged tag (green/red/grey). On mount POSTs the pre-seed 80c+20v fixtures so the panel is populated. `web/src/types.ts` adds `SphereWageOutcomeResponse`, `WageEffectAnalysisResponse`; `api.ts` adds `createWageEffectAnalysis`, `listWageEffectAnalyses`, `getWageEffectAnalysis`.
- **Deferred.** Wage change caused by a shift in the value of necessities — Part VI. Tendency of the rate of profit to fall — Part III.

### Vol. III Ch. 12 — what was built

`finance-service` extends `internal/avgprofit` for Capital Vol. III, Ch. 12 — **the last chapter of Part II**, which closes the conversion of profit into average profit. Two closing remarks plus a conservation summary: (1) a commodity's price of production changes for **only two reasons** — the general rate of profit changes (own value unchanged) or the commodity's own value changes (general rate unchanged); (2) a capitalist's claimed **grounds** for a higher price (slow turnover, risk, skill) create no new value — they are only a claim on a larger share of the aggregate surplus-value; (3) across the whole social capital, **total price of production = total value**, and for the average-composition capital surplus-value produced = average profit received (the conservation law of Ch. 9).

- **Domain types.** New file `services/finance-service/internal/avgprofit/price_change.go`: `PriceChangeCause` (`general_rate_change`/`individual_value_change`/`both`); `PriceOfProductionChangeID` + `NewPriceOfProductionChangeID()` + `IsZero()`; `PriceOfProductionChange` struct + `Validate()` (negative price → `ErrNegativeMagnitude`); `ComputePriceOfProductionChange` derives `RateChanged`/`ValueChanged`/`PriceChanged` from the cause; the `compensationActuallyIs` constant; `CompensationGround` value object + `ComputeCompensationGround` (`ActuallyIs` = "share in aggregate surplus-value", `CreatesValue` = false — for any reason). New file `part_two_summary.go`: `PartIISummaryID` + constructor; `AverageCompositionCheck`; `PartIISummary`; `ComputePartIISummary(spheres, prices, wageAnalyses)` — Σ values vs Σ prices (conservation), general rate from the first price (or `ComputeGeneralProfitRate(spheres)`), Σ surplus-value, Σ average profit = Σ(sphere.C·rate/10000). All reuse the Ch. 8/9 value types — no redeclarations.
- **Store.** `Store` interface gains `Create/Get PriceOfProductionChange`. `memory.go` adds one map; `mysql.go` adds INSERT/SELECT + `scanPriceOfProductionChange` over flat columns (booleans as `TINYINT(1)`). `CompensationGround` and `PartIISummary` are **not persisted** — `GetPartIISummary` aggregates the existing `ListProductionSpheres`/`ListPricesOfProduction`/`ListWageEffectAnalyses`. Migrations `00023_v3_ch12_price_change.sql` (table `price_of_production_changes`) and `00024_v3_ch12_seed.sql` (cotton general-rate-change `…1201`, linen value-change `…1202`).
- **HTTP endpoints (4).** `POST /v1/avgprofit/price-change` (400 bad json; 422 negative price; 201+Location); `GET /v1/avgprofit/price-change/{id}` (404 missing); `POST /v1/avgprofit/compensation-ground` (200 with the value object); `GET /v1/avgprofit/summary` (200 Part II snapshot). All proxied through api-gateway.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch12SupplementaryRemarks.tsx` (registered `v3-ch12`): a two-cause radio selector showing which flags fire and whether the price changed; a compensation-ground myth-buster (enter a reason → appears-to-be vs actually-is "share in aggregate surplus-value", creates-value no); a Part II summary panel loaded on mount with a Σ-values = Σ-prices conservation banner, general rate, surplus-value vs average-profit, and record counts. `web/src/types.ts` adds `PriceOfProductionChangeResponse`, `CompensationGroundResponse`, `PartIISummaryResponse`; `api.ts` adds `createPriceOfProductionChange`, `getPriceOfProductionChange`, `createCompensationGround`, `getPartIISummary`.
- **Part II complete.** With Ch. 12 done, all of Vol. III Part II (Ch. 8–12 — Conversion of Profit into Average Profit) is implemented.
- **Deferred.** The law of the tendency of the rate of profit to fall — Part III (Ch. 13–15).

### Vol. III Ch. 13 — what was built

`finance-service` opens **Part III** with a new bounded context, `internal/tendency` (`law.go`), for the law of the tendential fall in the rate of profit "as such". With the growing productivity of labour the **organic composition rises** (c grows relative to v); since v is the sole source of surplus-value, holding s′ constant means s = s′·v no longer keeps pace with C = c + v, so **p′ = s/(c+v) falls**. A dialectically opposed movement accompanies it: the **mass** of profit (C·p′) need not fall — as the rate falls the total capital grows, so the absolute mass can be preserved or even rise; at the limit, **absolute over-accumulation** is a capital increment that sets no additional living labour in motion.

- **Domain types.** `CompositionTrajectoryID` + `NewCompositionTrajectoryID()`; `RateMassContradictionID` + `NewRateMassContradictionID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex); `MassOfProfit`; `TrajectoryPeriod` (c, v, s, p′ — value object); `CompositionTrajectory` (persisted; `Periods` + derived `ProfitRates`) with `Validate()` and `DeriveProfitRates()`; `FallingProfitRateLaw` (single-period value object); `RateMassContradiction` (persisted) with `Validate()`; `AbsoluteOveraccumulation` (value object). Pure constructors `ComputeFallingProfitRateLaw`, `BuildCompositionTrajectory`, `ComputeRateMassContradiction`, `ComputeAbsoluteOveraccumulation`. Rates use the house **round-half-up** `profitRateBP` helper (Period 1: s=100, C=150 → 6667 bp), guarding divide-by-zero.
- **Store.** `Store` interface gains six methods (`Create/Get/List CompositionTrajectory`, `Create/Get/List RateMassContradiction`). `memory.go` adds two maps; `mysql.go` stores trajectory `Periods` as `periods_json TEXT` (Marshal on write, Unmarshal + re-derive `ProfitRates` on read via `scanCompositionTrajectory`), and rate-mass rows over flat columns (`scanRateMassContradiction`). Migrations `00025_v3_ch13_law.sql` (tables `composition_trajectories`, `rate_mass_contradictions`) and `00026_v3_ch13_seed.sql` (the five-period §law trajectory `…1301` with p′ 6667→2000; rate-mass equal-mass `…1302` and rising-mass `…1303`).
- **HTTP endpoints (5).** `POST /v1/tendency/trajectory` (400 bad json / empty steps; 422 negative magnitude; 201+Location), `GET /v1/tendency/trajectory` (list, `items` never null), `GET /v1/tendency/trajectory/{id}` (404 missing), `POST /v1/tendency/rate-mass` (201+Location), `GET /v1/tendency/rate-mass/{id}` (404 missing). All proxied through api-gateway under `/v1/tendency/`.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch13FallingProfitLaw.tsx` (registered `v3-ch13`): a trajectory builder (editable c/v step rows, s′ input) that tabulates each period's c, v, s, C, p′ with a falling-rate bar; a stored-trajectories list (seeded on a fresh volume); and a rate-vs-mass panel showing old/new mass and the signed Δ with prose ("the rate falls but the mass does not"). `web/src/types.ts` adds `TrajectoryPeriodResponse`, `CompositionTrajectoryResponse`, `RateMassContradictionResponse`; `api.ts` adds `createCompositionTrajectory`, `listCompositionTrajectories`, `getCompositionTrajectory`, `computeRateMassContradiction`, `getRateMassContradiction`.
- **Deferred.** Counteracting influences — Ch. 14. Internal contradictions of the law — Ch. 15.

### Vol. III Ch. 14 — what was built

`finance-service` **extends** the `internal/tendency` bounded context with a sibling file `counteracting.go` for the six counter-tendencies of Ch. 14. The same rising composition that drives p′ down also calls forth counter-effects that **hamper, retard, and partly paralyse** the fall. Five of the six are industrial forces that lift the rate of profit along the trajectory; the sixth, stock (interest-bearing) capital, is **excluded from the general rate** and contributes no uplift. The defining invariant: even with all five industrial forces active the law persists — the final rate of profit stays below the initial rate.

- **Domain types.** `CounteractingForceKind` (six consts §I–§VI: `intensified_exploitation`, `depressed_wages`, `cheaper_constant_capital`, `relative_overpopulation`, `foreign_trade`, `stock_capital`) with `IsValid()` and `Description()`; `CounteractingForceID` + `NewCounteractingForceID()` and `CounteractingScenarioID` + `NewCounteractingScenarioID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex); `CounteractingForce` (persisted; `ExcludedFromGeneralRate = Kind==KindStockCapital`) via `NewCounteractingForce`. Three value objects: `ExploitationIntensityEffect` (§I, Baseline/Modified p′), `ConstantCapitalCheapening` (§III, Old/New p′), `ForeignTradeEffect` (§V, derived signed `NetProfitRateEffect`). `CounteractingScenario` (persisted) applies a `[]CounteractingForce` to an embedded `CompositionTrajectory`; `BuildCounteractingScenario` adds a bounded per-period uplift (δ = s·weight/(c+v); weights §I/§II=500, §III/§V=1000, §IV=300, §VI=0) and enforces `FinalProfitRate < InitialProfitRate` (`ErrLawNotPreserved`). Rates use the existing round-half-up `profitRateBP`. New sentinels: `ErrInvalidForceKind`, `ErrModifiedRateNotHigher`, `ErrNewRateNotHigher`, `ErrInvalidCheapeningFactor`, `ErrEmptyForces`, `ErrLawNotPreserved`.
- **Store.** `Store` interface gains five methods (`Create/Get CounteractingForce`, `Create/Get/List CounteractingScenario`). `memory.go` adds two maps; `mysql.go` stores `forces`, `base_trajectory`, and `modified_rates` as JSON TEXT (`scanCounteractingForce`, `scanCounteractingScenario`). Migrations `00027_v3_ch14_counteracting.sql` (tables `counteracting_forces`, `counteracting_scenarios`) and `00028_v3_ch14_seed.sql` (one force per kind `…1401`–`…1406`; the all-five-industrial-forces scenario `…1407` with modified rates `8865→6650→4431→3325→2660`, final 2660 < initial 8865). Down deletes only those seed IDs.
- **HTTP endpoints (5).** `POST /v1/tendency/counteracting-force` (400 bad json; 422 invalid kind; 201+Location), `GET /v1/tendency/counteracting-force/{id}` (404 missing), `POST /v1/tendency/scenario` (400 bad json / empty force_kinds / empty steps; 422 invalid kind or law-not-preserved; 201+Location), `GET /v1/tendency/scenario` (list, `items` never null), `GET /v1/tendency/scenario/{id}` (404 missing). All proxied through api-gateway under `/v1/tendency/`.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch14CounteractingForces.tsx` (registered `v3-ch14`): six force checkboxes (the five industrial ones default-checked, stock capital flagged "excluded from general rate"), a c/v trajectory step builder with an s′ input, and a base-vs-modified p′ table with per-period uplift plus a "law retarded not abolished" summary that highlights `FinalProfitRate < InitialProfitRate`; a stored-scenarios list (seeded on a fresh volume). `web/src/types.ts` adds `CounteractingForceKind`, `CounteractingForceResponse`, `CounteractingScenarioResponse`; `api.ts` adds `createCounteractingForce`, `getCounteractingForce`, `createCounteractingScenario`, `listCounteractingScenarios`, `getCounteractingScenario`.
- **Deferred.** Internal contradictions of the law — Ch. 15.

### Vol. III Ch. 15 — Internal Contradictions of the Law

**Completes Part III.** `finance-service` **extends** the `internal/tendency` bounded context a third time with a sibling file `contradictions.go`. Ch. 13 stated the law; Ch. 14 retarded it; Ch. 15 exposes the contradiction it carries — the same rising composition that lowers the rate of profit also swells the mass of profit (§I), floods the market with unrealisable commodities (§II), piles up capital that finds no profitable employment (§III), and accelerates the concentration and centralisation of capital (§IV). The contradiction is resolved only violently, in **crisis**: a mass writedown of constant capital that momentarily restores the rate of profit.

- **Domain types.** `ContradictionKind` (four consts §I–§IV: `falling_rate_rising_mass`, `overproduction_underconsumption`, `overaccumulation_relative_overpopulation`, `concentration_centralisation`) with `IsValid()` and `Description()`; `InternalContradictionID` + `NewInternalContradictionID()` and `CrisisID` + `NewCrisisID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `InternalContradiction` (persisted) via `NewInternalContradiction`, which sets `IsCoexistent=true` for every valid kind. Four value objects: `Overproduction` (§II, `UnrealisedValue()`), `Overaccumulation` (§III, `IsAbsolute()`, `ShortfallBP()`), `CapitalConcentration` (§IV, iterative `FinalC()` / `Path()` — `C ← C + ⌊C·s′/10000⌋` per period), `CapitalCentralisation` (§IV, `ResultingCapital()` = absorbed × size). `Crisis` (persisted) via `NewCrisis`, deriving `PostCrisisProfitRate = roundHalfUp(pre·10000 / (10000 − writedown·100))` (reuses the round-half-up convention; always > pre for writedown ∈ (0,99)). `PartIIISummary` value object closes the part. New sentinels: `ErrInvalidContradictionKind`, `ErrInvalidWritedown` (writedown ∈ (0,100) exclusive), `ErrInvalidPeriods` (≥1).
- **Store.** `Store` interface gains six methods (`Create/Get/List Crisis`, `Create/Get/List InternalContradiction`). `memory.go` adds two maps; `mysql.go` stores all-scalar columns (`scanCrisis`, `scanInternalContradiction`), with the derived `post_crisis_profit_rate` persisted for query. Migrations `00029_v3_ch15_contradictions.sql` (tables `crises`, `internal_contradictions`) and `00030_v3_ch15_seed.sql` (three crises `…1501`–`…1503` with restored rates 1429 / 1000 / 1200; one contradiction per kind `…1504`–`…1507`). Down deletes only those seed IDs.
- **HTTP endpoints (6).** `POST /v1/tendency/crisis` (400 bad json; 422 invalid writedown / negative pre; 201+Location), `GET /v1/tendency/crisis` (list, `items` never null), `GET /v1/tendency/crisis/{id}` (404 missing), `POST /v1/tendency/contradiction` (400 bad json; 422 invalid kind; 201+Location), `GET /v1/tendency/contradiction/{id}` (404 missing), `GET /v1/tendency/summary` (assembles `PartIIISummary` from the four Part III list calls — trajectory/scenario/crisis/contradiction counts + latest of each). All proxied through api-gateway under `/v1/tendency/`.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch15InternalContradictions.tsx` (registered `v3-ch15`): a §I–§IV contradiction taxonomy with per-kind "Record" buttons, a crisis form (writedown % + pre-crisis bp → live restored-p′ preview, POST, crisis table with Δ), client-side concentration (compounding path) and centralisation (fused total) calculators mirroring the Go arithmetic, and a "Part III Complete" summary panel with per-chapter counts and latest records. `web/src/types.ts` adds `ContradictionKind`, `InternalContradictionResponse`, `CrisisResponse`, `CapitalConcentrationResponse`, `CapitalCentralisationResponse`, `PartIIISummaryResponse`; `api.ts` adds `createCrisis`, `listCrises`, `getCrisis`, `createContradiction`, `getContradiction`, `getPartIIISummary`. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx` with the quote "The real barrier of capitalist production is capital itself."
- **Part III closed.** With Chs. 13–15 merged, Part III — The Law of the Tendential Fall in the Rate of Profit — is complete: the law as such, its counteracting influences, and its internal contradictions.

### Vol. III Ch. 16 — Commercial Capital

**Opens Part IV.** `finance-service` gains a new bounded context `internal/merchant/` with `commercial_capital.go`. Commercial capital (merchant's capital) is money-capital and commodity-capital separated off from the industrial circuit and made independent — M—C—M′ — its exclusive function being the realisation of surplus-value already produced. The central invariant: `SurplusValueProduced == 0` in all four functions.

- **Domain types.** `CommercialCapitalFunction` (named int type; four consts: `FunctionRealisation=0`, `FunctionStorage=1`, `FunctionTransport=2`, `FunctionDistribution=3`). `CommercialCapitalID` + `NewCommercialCapitalID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `CommercialCapital` (persisted entity) via `NewCommercialCapital(moneyAdvanced, description, fn)` — returns `ErrNonPositiveMoney` if moneyAdvanced ≤ 0; sets `SurplusValueProduced=0`. `MerchantCircuit` (value object, M—C—M′) via `NewMerchantCircuit` — `Profit()` = M′−M; `Validate()` returns `ErrNonPositiveMoney` or `ErrNoProfit`. `CommodityCapitalForm` (value object, c+v+s decomposition, SourceCapital == "industrial") via `NewCommodityCapitalForm` — `TotalValue()` = c+v+s; `Validate()` returns `ErrNonIndustrialSource` or `ErrNegativeMagnitude`. `LinenMerchantExample` (seed fixture) via `LinenMerchant()` — £3,000 / 30,000 yards / 10p per yard. Sentinel errors: `ErrNonPositiveMoney`, `ErrNoProfit`, `ErrNonIndustrialSource`, `ErrNegativeMagnitude`.
- **Store.** `Store` interface gains three methods (`CreateCommercialCapital`, `GetCommercialCapital`, `ListCommercialCapitals`). `memory.go` adds `commercialCapitals map`. `mysql.go` adds three methods + `scanCommercialCapital` (all-scalar scan). Migrations `00031_v3_ch16_commercial_capital.sql` (table `commercial_capitals`) and `00032_v3_ch16_seed.sql` (three seed records `5eed000000000000001601`–`5eed000000000000001603`: linen merchant £3,000/realisation, industrial handoff £600/realisation, storage variant £1,500/storage). Down deletes only those seed IDs.
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/merchant/commercial-capital` (400 bad JSON; 422 ErrNonPositiveMoney; 201+Location), `GET /v1/merchant/commercial-capital` (list, `items` never null), `GET /v1/merchant/commercial-capital/{id}` (404 missing). Proxied through api-gateway under `/v1/merchant/`.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch16CommercialCapital.tsx` (registered `v3-ch16`): linen-merchant fixture display, four-functions taxonomy, M—C—M′ profit calculator (client-side), create-form (money_advanced / description / function select), and records table (money advanced, commodity, function, s-produced always 0). `web/src/types.ts` adds `CommercialCapitalFunction`, `CommercialCapitalResponse`, `MerchantCircuitResponse`; `api.ts` adds `createCommercialCapital`, `listCommercialCapitals`, `getCommercialCapital`. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Vol. III Ch. 17 — Commercial Profit

`finance-service` adds `internal/merchant/commercial_profit.go` as a sibling to `commercial_capital.go`. Commercial profit is not new value — it is a share of industrial surplus-value transferred to the merchant in proportion to their capital advance.

- **Domain types.** `CommercialProfitID` + `NewCommercialProfitID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `CommercialProfit` (persisted entity) via `NewCommercialProfit(productiveCapital, commercialCapital, surplusValue int64)` — returns `ErrNonPositiveCapital` if either capital ≤ 0, `ErrNegativeSurplusValue` if surplus < 0; computes `UnadjustedRateBP = roundHalfUp(s×10000, productive)` and `AdjustedRateBP = roundHalfUp(s×10000, productive+commercial)`; `NewValueCreated = 0` always. Five value objects: `AdjustedGeneralRate` (same two rates, not persisted), `SurplusValueDeduction` (MerchantShare = roundHalfUp(total×commercial, total_capital); conservation invariant), `CommercialWorker` (NecessaryLabourMinutes, WorkdayMinutes, UnpaidLabourMinutes = workday−necessary; NewValueCreated = 0), `UnpaidCommercialLabour` (Minutes ≥ 0; NewValueCreated = 0), `WholesaleRetailSpread` (SpreadPence = retail−wholesale). Package-local `basisPoints = 10000` const and `roundHalfUp` helper. Sentinel errors: `ErrNonPositiveCapital`, `ErrNegativeSurplusValue`, `ErrNegativeLabourMinutes`, `ErrCommercialCapitalExceedsTotal`.
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateCommercialProfit`, `GetCommercialProfit`, `ListCommercialProfits`). `memory.go` adds `commercialProfits map`. `mysql.go` adds three methods + `scanCommercialProfit` (all-scalar). Migrations `00033_v3_ch17_commercial_profit.sql` (table `commercial_profits`) and `00034_v3_ch17_seed.sql` (three seed records `5eed000000000000001701`–`5eed000000000000001703`: productive=720000/commercial=180000/surplus=144000 → 2000/1600 bp; half-surplus variant → 1000/800 bp; 800k/100k variant → 2000/1778 bp).
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/merchant/commercial-profit` (400 bad JSON; 422 ErrNonPositiveCapital/ErrNegativeSurplusValue; 201+Location), `GET /v1/merchant/commercial-profit` (list, `items` never null), `GET /v1/merchant/commercial-profit/{id}` (404 missing). Proxied through api-gateway.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch17CommercialProfit.tsx` (registered `v3-ch17`, status flipped pending→done): Marx Ch.17 fixture display (unadjusted 20% / adjusted 16%), commercial-clerk unpaid-labour panel (14400/28800/14400 min, NewValueCreated=0), create-form with live rate preview, records table. `web/src/types.ts` adds `CommercialProfitResponse`, `AdjustedGeneralRateResponse`, `CommercialWorkerResponse`, `WholesaleRetailSpreadResponse`; `api.ts` adds `createCommercialProfit`, `listCommercialProfits`, `getCommercialProfit`. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Vol. III Ch. 18 — Merchant Turnover

`finance-service` adds `internal/merchant/merchant_turnover.go` as a sibling to `commercial_profit.go`. The annual profit on merchant's capital is invariant to turnover count and units: `AnnualMerchantProfit = roundHalfUp(moneyAdvanced × generalRateBP, 10000)`. Faster turnover reduces the per-unit markup: `MarkupPerUnit = AnnualMerchantProfit / (TurnoverCount × UnitsPerTurnover)` (integer division — truncation).

- **Domain types.** `MerchantTurnoverID` + `NewMerchantTurnoverID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `MerchantTurnover` (persisted entity) via `NewMerchantTurnover(moneyAdvanced, generalRateBP, turnoverCount, unitsPerTurnover int64)` — returns `ErrNonPositiveCapital` if moneyAdvanced or generalRateBP ≤ 0, `ErrInvalidTurnoverCount` if turnoverCount < 1, `ErrNoUnits` if unitsPerTurnover ≤ 0. `TurnoverPoint` and `TurnoverEffectAnalysis` value objects (not persisted): `NewTurnoverEffectAnalysis` computes markup at each supplied turnover count, demonstrating the inverse relationship. `MerchantPriceOfProduction` value object: `PriceOfProduction = CostPrice + AverageProfit`. Reuses package-level `basisPoints` and `roundHalfUp` from `commercial_profit.go`.
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateMerchantTurnover`, `GetMerchantTurnover`, `ListMerchantTurnovers`). `memory.go` adds `turnoversM map`. `mysql.go` adds three methods + `scanMerchantTurnover`. Migrations `00035_v3_ch18_merchant_turnover.sql` (table `merchant_turnovers`) and `00036_v3_ch18_seed.sql` (three seed records `5eed000000000000001801`–`5eed000000000000001803`: capital=100000p/rate=1500bp → annual=15000p; turnover×1→markup=50p, turnover×5→markup=10p, turnover×3→markup=16p (integer div)).
- **HTTP endpoints (4).** `POST /v1/merchant/turnover` (400 bad JSON; 422 domain errors; 201+Location), `GET /v1/merchant/turnover` (list, `items` never null), `GET /v1/merchant/turnover/{id}` (404 missing), `POST /v1/merchant/turnover-effect` (pure compute, not persisted; 200 with TurnoverEffectAnalysis). Proxied through api-gateway (`/v1/merchant/turnover`, `/v1/merchant/turnover/{rest...}`, `/v1/merchant/turnover-effect` each wired separately — the wildcard does not cover the sibling path).
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch18MerchantTurnover.tsx` (registered `v3-ch18`, status flipped pending→done): Marx Ch.18 fixture display (×1→50p/unit, ×5→10p/unit, annual profit invariant at £150), turnover-effect compare panel (comma-separated counts → POST → table showing markup shrinking as turnover rises), create-form with live annual-profit and markup preview, records table. `web/src/types.ts` adds `MerchantTurnoverResponse`, `TurnoverPointResponse`, `TurnoverEffectAnalysisResponse`, `MerchantPriceOfProductionResponse`; `api.ts` adds `createMerchantTurnover`, `listMerchantTurnovers`, `getMerchantTurnover`, `computeTurnoverEffect`. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Vol. III Ch. 19 — Money-Dealing Capital

`finance-service` adds `internal/merchant/money_dealing.go` as a sibling to `merchant_turnover.go`. Money-dealing capital performs the purely technical operations of monetary circulation (receipt, payment, exchange, safekeeping, bookkeeping) that arise necessarily from commodity circulation. Like commercial capital it earns `MoneyDealingProfit = roundHalfUp(moneyAdvanced × generalRateBP, basisPoints)` from the general rate of profit, but `NewValueCreated == 0` always — it creates no surplus-value.

- **Domain types.** `MoneyOperationKind int` (const 1–5: KindReceipt/KindPayment/KindExchange/KindSafekeeping/KindBookkeeping; `IsValid() bool`). `ErrInvalidOperationKind` new sentinel. `MoneyDealingCapitalID` + `NewMoneyDealingCapitalID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `MoneyDealingCapital` (persisted entity) via `NewMoneyDealingCapital(moneyAdvanced, generalRateBP int64, operationKinds []MoneyOperationKind)` — `ErrNonPositiveMoney` if moneyAdvanced ≤ 0, `ErrNonPositiveCapital` if generalRateBP ≤ 0, `ErrInvalidOperationKind` if any kind invalid; `OperationKinds` stored as JSON array. `CashReserve` value object (`NewCashReserve(amount) → ErrNegativeMagnitude if amount < 0`). `MoneyDealingCircuit` value object (M—M′ circuit; `Profit() = MoneyReturned − MoneyAdvanced`; `NewMoneyDealingCircuit → ErrNonPositiveMoney / ErrNoProfit`). `MoneyOperation` value object (Kind + Description; used in API/tests, not persisted). Reuses `basisPoints`, `roundHalfUp`, `ErrNonPositiveMoney`, `ErrNonPositiveCapital`, `ErrNegativeMagnitude`, `ErrNoProfit` from sibling files.
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateMoneyDealingCapital`, `GetMoneyDealingCapital`, `ListMoneyDealingCapitals`). `memory.go` adds `moneyDealingCapitals map`. `mysql.go` adds three methods + `scanMoneyDealingCapital` (scans `operation_kinds` MEDIUMTEXT column via `json.Unmarshal` + per-kind `IsValid()` check; INSERT marshals `[]MoneyOperationKind` to JSON string). `mysql.go` import group gains `"fmt"`. Migrations `00037_v3_ch19_money_dealing.sql` (table `money_dealing_capitals`) and `00038_v3_ch19_seed.sql` (three seed records `5eed000000000000001901`–`5eed000000000000001903`: operations [1,2,3]/[4]/[3,5] at 1500 bp → profits 75000/30000/45000p).
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/merchant/money-dealing` (400 bad JSON; 422 ErrNonPositiveMoney/ErrNonPositiveCapital/ErrInvalidOperationKind; 201+Location), `GET /v1/merchant/money-dealing` (list, `items` never null), `GET /v1/merchant/money-dealing/{id}` (404 missing). Proxied through api-gateway (`/v1/merchant/money-dealing`, `/v1/merchant/money-dealing/{rest...}`).
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch19MoneyDealingCapital.tsx` (registered `v3-ch19`, status flipped pending→done): Marx Ch.19 fixture display (M=£5,000, profit=£750, new value=£0), create-form with checkbox multi-select for the five operation kinds and live profit preview, records table showing operation kinds + profit + new-value-created=0. `web/src/types.ts` adds `MoneyOperationKind`, `MoneyDealingCapitalResponse`, `MoneyDealingCircuitResponse`; `api.ts` adds `createMoneyDealingCapital`, `listMoneyDealingCapitals`, `getMoneyDealingCapital`. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Vol. III Ch. 21 — Interest-Bearing Capital

`finance-service` adds a new `internal/credit/` package. **This chapter opens Part V.** Interest-bearing capital is money advanced as M—M′: the money-capitalist lends MoneyAdvanced at InterestRateBP for LoanTermDays, earning InterestEarned = roundHalfUp(MoneyAdvanced × InterestRateBP, 10000). MoneyReturned = MoneyAdvanced + InterestEarned. The interest-bearing form creates no new value (NewValueCreated == 0 always); it redistributes a portion of average surplus-value from the functioning capitalist (borrower) to the money-capitalist (lender). The functioning capitalist retains the profit of enterprise.

- **Domain types (`credit` package).** Own `basisPoints`/`roundHalfUp` helpers (copied pattern; not imported from `merchant`). Sentinels `ErrNonPositiveMoney`, `ErrNonPositiveRate`, `ErrNonPositiveTerm`, `ErrInterestExceedsProfit`. `InterestBearingCapitalID` + `NewInterestBearingCapitalID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `InterestBearingCapital` (persisted entity) via `NewInterestBearingCapital(moneyAdvanced, interestRateBP, loanTermDays int64)`. Value objects: `MoneyCapitalCircuit` (invariant MoneyReturned==MoneyAdvanced+InterestEarned), `LoanableCapital` (>0), `Interest` with `NewInterest(value, avgProfit)` (rejects value≥avgProfit — interest is a portion of profit), `LoanTerm` (days>0), `MoneyCapitalist` (EarnsInterest=true), `FunctioningCapitalist` (EarnsInterest=false).
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateInterestBearingCapital`, `GetInterestBearingCapital`, `ListInterestBearingCapitals`). `memory.go` adds `interestBearingCapitals map`. `mysql.go` adds three methods + `scanInterestBearingCapital`. Migrations `00041_v3_ch21_interest_bearing_capital.sql` (table `interest_bearing_capitals`) and `00042_v3_ch21_seed.sql` (two seed records `5eed000000000000002100`–`5eed000000000000002101`: £1,000@5%/365 days and £5,000@15%/180 days).
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/credit/interest-bearing-capital` (400 bad JSON; 422 ErrNonPositiveMoney/ErrNonPositiveRate/ErrNonPositiveTerm; 201+Location), `GET /v1/credit/interest-bearing-capital` (list, `items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/interest-bearing-capital/{id}` (404 missing). Proxied through api-gateway (`/v1/credit/interest-bearing-capital`, `/v1/credit/interest-bearing-capital/{rest...}`).
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch21InterestBearingCapital.tsx` (registered `v3-ch21`, status flipped pending→done): M—M′ circuit exemplars, create-form with live preview of interest earned and money returned, stored-loans list. `web/src/types.ts` adds `InterestBearingCapital`, `MoneyCapitalCircuit`; `api.ts` adds `createInterestBearingCapital`, `listInterestBearingCapitals`, `getInterestBearingCapital`. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Vol. III Ch. 22 — Rate of Interest

`finance-service` extends the `internal/credit/` package opened by Ch. 21. The rate of interest has no "natural" or law-governed level — it is determined empirically by supply and demand in the money market. Its maximum is the average profit rate; its minimum approaches but never reaches zero. The chapter introduces `IndustrialCyclePhase` (5-phase enum) to capture the cyclical behaviour of interest rates and `InterestRateAnalysis` (compute-only) for splitting average profit into interest and profit of enterprise.

- **Domain types (`credit` package).** New sentinels `ErrNegativeRate`, `ErrNonPositiveAverageProfit`, `ErrRateExceedsAverageProfit`, `ErrInvalidCyclePhase`. `IndustrialCyclePhase int` (constants `PhaseInactivity=1` … `PhaseCrisis=5`; `IsValid() bool`). `RateOfInterestID` + `NewRateOfInterestID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `RateOfInterest` (persisted entity) via `NewRateOfInterest(rateBP, avgProfitRateBP int64, phase IndustrialCyclePhase, period string)` — rejects rateBP<0, avgBP<=0, rateBP>=avgBP, invalid phase. `InterestRateAnalysis` (compute-only, not persisted) via `NewInterestRateAnalysis(avgProfitRateBP, interestRateBP int64)` — `ProfitOfEnterpriseBP = avg - interest`. Value objects: `InterestRateBound` (MaxBP==avg, MinBP>=0, MinBP<=MaxBP), `MarketInterestRate` (RateBP>=0). Reuses package-local `basisPoints`/`roundHalfUp` from `interest_bearing_capital.go`.
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateRateOfInterest`, `GetRateOfInterest`, `ListRatesOfInterest`). `memory.go` adds `ratesOfInterest map` with newest-first sort (createdAt DESC, id ASC tiebreak). `mysql.go` adds three methods + `scanRateOfInterest`. Migrations `00043_v3_ch22_rate_of_interest.sql` (table `rate_of_interests`) and `00044_v3_ch22_seed.sql` (three seed records `5eed000000000000002201`–`5eed000000000000002203`: canonical avg-20%/interest-500bp, prosperity 1843 interest-150bp, crisis 1847 interest-800bp).
- **HTTP endpoints (4).** `POST /v1/credit/rate-of-interest` (400 bad JSON; 422 on all four sentinels; 201+Location), `GET /v1/credit/rate-of-interest` (`items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/rate-of-interest/{id}` (404 missing), `POST /v1/credit/interest-rate-analysis` (stateless compute; 422 on validation; 200+`InterestRateAnalysis`). Proxied through existing api-gateway `/v1/credit/` rule — no gateway change needed.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch22RateOfInterest.tsx` (registered `v3-ch22`, status pending→done, circuitNode `["M","M-prime"]`): seed exemplars showing avg/interest/enterprise split and cycle phase badge; stateless compute form (profit of enterprise); record-observation form with live preview; stored-observations list. `web/src/types.ts` adds `RateOfInterest`, `InterestRateAnalysis`, `InterestRateBound`; `api.ts` adds `createRateOfInterest`, `listRatesOfInterest`, `getRateOfInterest`, `computeInterestRateAnalysis`. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Vol. III Ch. 23 — Interest and Profit of Enterprise

`finance-service` extends the `internal/credit/` package. The total profit of capital (= average profit) is divided into two qualitatively distinct categories: interest (paid to the money-capitalist as owner) and profit of enterprise (retained by the functioning capitalist as active user). This division mystifies the surplus-value relation: the functioning capitalist appears to *work*, the money-capitalist merely *owns*. The chapter introduces `ProfitDivision` (persisted entity), `CapitalOwnershipForm` (enum), `WagesOfSuperintendence` (value object), and `MystificationIndex` (degree of concealment in bp).

- **Domain types (`credit` package).** New sentinels `ErrNonPositiveTotalProfit`, `ErrNegativeInterest`, `ErrInterestNotLessThanTotal`, `ErrInvalidOwnershipForm`. `CapitalOwnershipForm int` (constants `FormOwnerOperator=1`, `FormSeparated=2`; `IsValid() bool`). `MystificationIndex int64` (bp [0,10000]). `WagesOfSuperintendence` (value object; `LabourMinutes int64`). `ProfitOfEnterprise` (value object; `ProfitOfEnterpriseBP = total - interest`) via `NewProfitOfEnterprise(totalProfitBP, interestBP int64)`. `ProfitDivisionID` + `NewProfitDivisionID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `ProfitOfEnterpriseID` + `NewProfitOfEnterpriseID()`. `ProfitDivision` (persisted entity) via `NewProfitDivision(totalProfitBP, interestBP int64, ownershipForm CapitalOwnershipForm, wages WagesOfSuperintendence, mystification MystificationIndex)` — derives `ProfitOfEnterpriseBP = total - interest`; rejects totalBP<=0, interestBP<0, interestBP>=totalBP, invalid form.
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateProfitDivision`, `GetProfitDivision`, `ListProfitDivisions`). `memory.go` adds `profitDivisions map` with newest-first sort (createdAt DESC, id ASC tiebreak). `mysql.go` adds three methods + `scanProfitDivision`. Migrations `00045_v3_ch23_profit_of_enterprise.sql` (table `profit_divisions`) and `00046_v3_ch23_seed.sql` (two seed records `5eed000000000000002301`–`5eed000000000000002302`: borrowed capital 2000/500→1500 FormSeparated; own capital 2000/0→2000 FormOwnerOperator).
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/credit/profit-division` (400 bad JSON; 422 on all four sentinels; 201+Location), `GET /v1/credit/profit-division` (`items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/profit-division/{id}` (404 missing). Proxied through existing api-gateway `/v1/credit/` rule — no gateway change needed.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch23ProfitOfEnterprise.tsx` (registered `v3-ch23`, status pending→done, circuitNode `["M","M-prime"]`, part 5): seed exemplars showing total/interest/enterprise split and ownership-form badge; record-division form with live preview (Math.floor integer subtraction); stored-records list. `web/src/types.ts` adds `ProfitDivision`, `WagesOfSuperintendence`; `api.ts` adds `createProfitDivision`, `listProfitDivisions`, `getProfitDivision`. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Vol. III Ch. 24 — Externalisation / Fetish Capital

`finance-service` extends the `internal/credit/` package. Interest-bearing capital in the form M—M' is the most fetishised form of capital: money appears to breed money automatically, erasing all trace of production and surplus-labour. The compound-interest formula `s = c(1+i)^n` captures this fetish mathematically. Dr. Price's fantasy (1 penny at 5% compound for 1776 years) and Pitt's sinking fund illustrate the absurdity of mistaking arithmetic for economic law. The qualitative limit is the working day: surplus-value is bounded by living labour, not by a mathematical formula.

- **Domain types (`credit` package).** New sentinels `ErrNonPositivePrincipal`, `ErrNonPositiveRateBP`, `ErrNonPositivePeriodYears`. `FetishForm int` (constants `FormSimpleInterest=1`, `FormCompoundInterest=2`, `FormMM=3`). `AccumulationLimit` (value object; `WorkdayMinutes int64 >= 0`). `CompoundInterestID` + `NewCompoundInterestID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `CompoundInterestSchedule` (persisted entity; `Principal`, `RateBP`, `PeriodYears`, `FinalValue` int64; `NewValueCreated int64` always 0; `FetishForm`) via `NewCompoundInterestSchedule(principal, rateBP, periodYears int64, form FetishForm)` — derives `FinalValue` by integer iteration: start v=principal; repeat periodYears times: `v = roundHalfUp(v*(10000+rateBP), 10000)`. Reuses `basisPoints`/`roundHalfUp` from `interest_bearing_capital.go`.
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateCompoundInterestSchedule`, `GetCompoundInterestSchedule`, `ListCompoundInterestSchedules`). `memory.go` adds `compoundInterestSchedules map` with newest-first sort (createdAt DESC, id ASC tiebreak). `mysql.go` adds three methods + `scanCompoundInterestSchedule`. Migrations `00047_v3_ch24_compound_interest.sql` (table `compound_interest_schedules`) and `00048_v3_ch24_seed.sql` (two seed records `5eed000000000000002400`–`5eed000000000000002401`: Dr. Price 10000u@5%×100yr FinalValue=1315126; Pitt £250k@5%×10yr FinalValue=407224).
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/credit/compound-interest` (400 bad JSON; 422 on all three sentinels; 201+Location), `GET /v1/credit/compound-interest` (`items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/compound-interest/{id}` (404 missing). Proxied through existing api-gateway `/v1/credit/` rule — no gateway change needed.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch24CompoundInterest.tsx` (registered `v3-ch24`, status done, circuitNode `["M","M-prime"]`, part 5): Dr. Price and Pitt seed exemplars; calculator form with live preview (Math.floor integer iteration); stored schedules list showing fetish-form badge and new_value_created=0. `web/src/types.ts` adds `CompoundInterestSchedule`; `api.ts` adds `createCompoundInterestSchedule`, `listCompoundInterestSchedules`, `getCompoundInterestSchedule`. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Vol. III Ch. 25 — Credit and Fictitious Capital

`finance-service` extends the `internal/credit/` package with `fictitious_capital.go`. The credit system develops from money's function as a means of payment: a **bill of exchange** circulates as commercial money before its maturity date (in 1839, such bills were the majority of British currency). **Fictitious capital** arises when an income stream is capitalised at the prevailing rate of interest — a government consol of £5/year at 5% trades as £100 of "capital" even though the original loan was consumed long ago; only the claim on future taxes persists. A share is a title to future surplus-value, not the underlying real capital.

- **Domain types (`credit` package).** New sentinels `ErrNonPositiveFaceValue`, `ErrNonPositiveMaturityDays`, `ErrNonPositiveAnnualIncome`, `ErrNonPositiveRateBPFict`. `FictitiousKind int` (constants `KindBill=1`, `KindBankNote=2`, `KindPublicDebt=3`, `KindShare=4`). `BillOfExchangeID` + `NewBillOfExchangeID()` and `FictitiousCapitalID` + `NewFictitiousCapitalID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `BillOfExchange` (persisted entity; `Drawer`, `Drawee` strings; `FaceValue`, `MaturityDays int64`; `Description`) via `NewBillOfExchange(...)` — `FaceValue > 0` and `MaturityDays > 0` invariants. `FictitiousCapital` (persisted entity; `Kind`, `Name`, `AnnualIncome`, `RateBP`, `CapitalisedValue int64`; `RealCapitalExists bool`; `Description`) via `NewFictitiousCapital(kind, name, annualIncome, rateBP, description)` — derives `CapitalisedValue = roundHalfUp(annualIncome*basisPoints, rateBP)` and `RealCapitalExists = (kind != KindPublicDebt)`. Reuses `basisPoints`/`roundHalfUp` from `interest_bearing_capital.go`.
- **Store.** Interface gains six methods (`CreateBillOfExchange`/`GetBillOfExchange`/`ListBillsOfExchange`, `CreateFictitiousCapital`/`GetFictitiousCapital`/`ListFictitiousCapitals`). `memory.go` adds `billsOfExchange` + `fictitiousCapitals` maps with newest-first sort (createdAt DESC, id ASC tiebreak). `mysql.go` adds the six methods + `scanBillOfExchange` + `scanFictitiousCapital`. Migrations `00049_v3_ch25_fictitious_capital.sql` (tables `bills_of_exchange`, `fictitious_capitals`) and `00050_v3_ch25_seed.sql` (seed IDs `5eed000000000000002500`–`...2503`: 1839 UK bills £132m/90d; 1840s clearing house £3m settled with £200k/1d; UK Consol £5@5%→£100 consumed; Railway Share £10@5%→£200 real).
- **HTTP endpoints (6).** `POST /v1/credit/bills-of-exchange` (400 bad JSON; 422 on face-value/maturity sentinels; 201+Location), `GET /v1/credit/bills-of-exchange` (`items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/bills-of-exchange/{id}` (404 missing); the same trio for `/v1/credit/fictitious-capital` (422 on income/rate sentinels). New api-gateway proxy rules for both prefixes; the same pass also backfilled the previously-missing Ch. 22–24 credit gateway rules (`rate-of-interest`, `interest-rate-analysis`, `profit-division`, `compound-interest`), which had no gateway entry.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch25CreditFictitiousCapital.tsx` (registered `v3-ch25`, status done, circuitNode `["delta-M"]`, part 5): consol and railway-share exemplars; capitalisation calculator with live preview (`Math.round` mirrors server round-half-up) of `capitalised_value` and derived `real_capital_exists`; stored fictitious-capital list with kind badge and consumed/real tag; bill-of-exchange draw form; stored-bills list. `web/src/types.ts` adds `BillOfExchange`, `FictitiousCapital`; `api.ts` adds the six client functions. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`. (Also removed a stale duplicate `v3-ch24` registry row left by the Ch. 24 scaffold.)

### Ch. 27 — Role of Credit in Capitalist Production

`finance-service` adds `internal/credit/credit_role.go` implementing the three roles credit plays in capitalist production (Vol. III Ch. 27, Part V).

- **Domain types.** `CreditRole int` enum (1=`RoleEqualiseProfitRate`, 2=`RoleReduceCirculationCosts`, 3=`RoleStockCompanies`; `IsValid() bool`). New sentinels `ErrCapitalMismatch`, `ErrNonPositiveWage`, `ErrNotWorkerOwned`, `ErrInvalidCreditRole`. `Manager` value type (`WageMinutes int64 > 0`; wages of superintendence, NOT profit of enterprise). `StockCompanyID` + `NewStockCompanyID()` + `StockCompany` (persisted entity; invariant: `TotalCapital == FixedCapital + FloatingCapital`; `Manager.WageMinutes > 0`). `CooperativeFactoryID` + `NewCooperativeFactoryID()` + `CooperativeFactory` (persisted entity; invariant: `WorkerOwned == true`). `CreditFunctionAnalysisID` + `CirculationCostSaving` value type + `ComputeCirculationCostSaving(metallic, actual int64, desc string) CirculationCostSaving` pure function (Scottish banking: £27m−£3m=£24m). `CreditFunctionAnalysis` compute-only type (not persisted).
- **Store.** Interface gains six methods: `CreateStockCompany`/`GetStockCompany`/`ListStockCompanies`, `CreateCooperativeFactory`/`GetCooperativeFactory`/`ListCooperativeFactories`. `memory.go` adds `stockCompanies` + `cooperativeFactories` maps with newest-first sort (createdAt DESC, id ASC tiebreak). `mysql.go` adds the six methods + `scanStockCompany` (flattens `Manager` into `manager_name`, `manager_title`, `manager_wage_minutes` columns) + `scanCooperativeFactory`. Migrations `00053_v3_ch27_credit_role.sql` (tables `stock_companies`, `cooperative_factories`) and `00054_v3_ch27_seed.sql` (seed IDs `5eed...2700`–`2701`: United Alkali Trust £5m+£1m=£6m, South Eastern Railway £9m+£1m=£10m; `5eed...2710`–`2711`: Rochdale Pioneers 28 workers/£28 capital, Paisley Thread 120 workers).
- **HTTP endpoints (5).** `POST /v1/credit/stock-companies` (400 bad JSON; 422 ErrCapitalMismatch/ErrNonPositiveWage; 201+Location), `GET /v1/credit/stock-companies` (items never null), `GET /v1/credit/stock-companies/{id}` (404 missing). `POST /v1/credit/cooperative-factories` (422 ErrNotWorkerOwned; 201+Location), `GET /v1/credit/cooperative-factories` (items never null). Gateway proxy rules added for both prefixes (`/v1/credit/stock-companies`, `/v1/credit/cooperative-factories`).

### Ch. 32 — Money-Capital and Real Capital III

`finance-service` adds `internal/credit/real_capital_conclusion.go`, the **conclusion** of Marx's money-capital/real-capital trilogy (Vol. III Ch. 32, Part V). Accumulated loanable money-capital **always overstates real accumulation**: revenue intended for consumption passes temporarily through a money form and appears as loan capital though it is no new productive capital; capital **released** by falling input prices, merchants' idle money, and the savings of the growing **rentier class** all swell loan capital independently of any real expansion. A release reflects real accumulation **only when reinvested**. In a **crisis** the demand for loan capital is a demand for **means of payment** — convertibility into gold — **not** for productive capital, the distinction on which the banking theory that conflates the two collapses.

- **Domain types.** New sentinels `ErrNegativeReleasedAmount`, `ErrInvalidReleaseCause`, `ErrNonPositiveRevenue`, `ErrNegativeOverstatement`. `CapitalReleaseCause` int enum (`CauseInputPriceFall=1`, `CauseMerchantIdleMoney=2`, `CauseRentierSavings=3`, `CauseRevenuePassthrough=4`) with `IsValid()`. `LoanCapitalOverstatement` value struct (`OverstatementPct int64` basis points ≥ 0 — loan capital never understates real accumulation in the long run; `Description`). `RevenueLoanCapitalConversion` value type (`RevenueLoanCapitalConversionID`/`NewRevenueLoanCapitalConversionID()`; `RevenueAmount int64` > 0) via `NewRevenueLoanCapitalConversion(...)` (`ErrNonPositiveRevenue`). `MeansOfPaymentDemand` value type (`MeansOfPaymentDemandID`/`NewMeansOfPaymentDemandID()`; reuses `IndustrialCyclePhase`; `Amount int64`; `IsProductiveCapitalDemand bool`) via `NewMeansOfPaymentDemand(...)` which forces the flag to **false** in `PhaseCrisis`. `Rentier` value type (`Name`, `InterestIncome int64`, `Description`). `CapitalRelease` (the **only** persisted entity; `CapitalReleaseID`/`NewCapitalReleaseID()` 96-bit crypto/rand hex; `ReleasedAmount int64` ≥ 0, `Cause`, `Reinvested bool`, embedded `Overstatement`) via `NewCapitalRelease(...)` (`ErrNegativeReleasedAmount`, `ErrInvalidReleaseCause`, `ErrNegativeOverstatement`). `AccumulationVerdict()` pure fn names overstates vs. reflects real accumulation from `Reinvested`. `IndustrialCyclePhase`/`PhaseCrisis`/`ErrInvalidCyclePhase` reused from `rate_of_interest.go`.
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateCapitalRelease`/`GetCapitalRelease`/`ListCapitalReleases`). `memory.go` adds a `capitalReleases` map with newest-first sort (createdAt DESC, id ASC tiebreak); `mysql.go` adds the three methods + `scanCapitalRelease`. **Every** field — including both `LoanCapitalOverstatement` sub-fields (`overstatement_pct`, `overstatement_description`) — is a column and round-trips identically through Memory and MySQL. Migrations `00063_v3_ch32_real_capital_conclusion.sql` (table `capital_releases`) and `00064_v3_ch32_seed.sql` (seed IDs `5eed000000000000003200`–`3201`: capital released by raw-material price fall, not reinvested, overstates 25%; rentier-class savings, 100% apparent accumulation).
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/credit/capital-release` (400 bad JSON; 422 `ErrNegativeReleasedAmount`/`ErrInvalidReleaseCause`/`ErrNegativeOverstatement`; 201 + Location), `GET /v1/credit/capital-release` (`items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/capital-release/{id}` (404 missing). New api-gateway proxy rules for the path prefix plus its `{rest...}` variant.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch32MoneyRealCapitalIII.tsx` (registered `v3-ch32`, status pending→done, circuitNode `["M","delta-M"]`): explainer on loan capital always overstating real accumulation and means-of-payment demand in crisis; three exemplar cards (price-fall release, rentier savings, 1857 crisis); a capital-release form (amount, cause selector, reinvested checkbox, overstatement %) showing the live accumulation verdict mirroring the Go pure fn; live list of stored releases on mount and after create. `web/src/types.ts` adds `LoanCapitalOverstatement`, `MeansOfPaymentDemand`, `CapitalRelease`, and `CreateCapitalReleaseInput`; `api.ts` adds the three client functions. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`. **Completes** the Money-Capital/Real-Capital trilogy.

### Ch. 33 — Medium of Circulation in the Credit System

`finance-service` adds `internal/credit/circulation_credit.go` (Vol. III Ch. 33, Part V). Credit **governs currency velocity**: the same banknote circulates far faster when backed by bills of exchange and mutual bank claims, so the mass of actual coin in circulation is a small fraction of the commodity values it mediates. The **London Clearing House** makes this concrete — banks present mutual claims daily and net them; only the **net balance** is settled in actual money. In Marx's time, £3–4 million of claims were routinely cancelled with under £200,000 of coin (a saving above 90 per cent). The chapter's formal invariant: `MoneyUsed <= TotalClaims`; `NetBalance = TotalClaims − MoneyUsed`.

- **Domain types.** `ClearingHouseSettlement` (persisted entity; `ClearingHouseSettlementID`/`NewClearingHouseSettlementID()` 96-bit crypto/rand hex; `TotalClaims int64` > 0; `MoneyUsed int64` >= 0 and <= TotalClaims; `NetBalance int64` computed = TotalClaims − MoneyUsed) via `NewClearingHouseSettlement(...)` (`ErrNonPositiveTotalClaims`, `ErrNegativeMoneyUsed`, `ErrMoneyUsedExceedsClaims`). `CurrencyVelocity` pure-function value type (`ActualNotes`, `Velocity int64` >= 1, `EffectiveMoneySupply = ActualNotes × Velocity`) via `NewCurrencyVelocity(...)`. `CreditContraction` value type (qualitative: credit contracts in crisis; `ContractedBy int64` >= 0). `EffectiveMoneySupply` alias function mirrors the Marx fixture: £500 × 5 = £2,500.
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateClearingHouseSettlement`/`GetClearingHouseSettlement`/`ListClearingHouseSettlements`). `memory.go` adds a `clearingHouseSettlements` map with newest-first sort; `mysql.go` adds the three methods + `scanClearingHouseSettlement`. Migrations `00065_v3_ch33_circulation_credit.sql` (table `clearing_house_settlements`) and `00066_v3_ch33_seed.sql` (seed IDs `5eed000000000000003300`–`3301`: London Clearing House 1839 — £400m claims settled with £22m; Bank Clearing 1857 crisis — contraction).
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/credit/clearing-house-settlements` (400 bad JSON; 422 on invariant violations; 201 + Location), `GET /v1/credit/clearing-house-settlements` (`items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/clearing-house-settlements/{id}` (404 missing). New api-gateway proxy rules for the path prefix.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch33CirculationCredit.tsx` (registered `v3-ch33`, status pending→done, circuitNode `["M","delta-M"]`): explainer on credit governing velocity and the clearing house; a client-side currency-velocity calculator (actualNotes × velocity → effective money supply, with the Marx £500×5=£2,500 fixture); a clearing-house settlement form (name, description, total_claims, money_used) showing the live money-saved percentage and the returned net_balance; live list of stored settlements on mount and after create. `web/src/types.ts` adds `ClearingHouseSettlement` and `CreateClearingHouseSettlementInput`; `api.ts` adds the three client functions. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Ch. 34 — Currency Principle and Bank Act of 1844

`finance-service` adds `internal/credit/note_issue_constraint.go` (Vol. III Ch. 34, Part V). The **Currency School** (Overstone, Torrens, Norman) held that banknotes must behave like gold coin: a gold drain must trigger an equal note contraction. **Peel's Bank Act of 1844** split the Bank of England into separate Issue and Banking departments, fixing note issuance to gold reserves plus a £14 million fiduciary limit. Marx demonstrates that the Act **intensified crises** rather than prevented them — it had to be suspended in both 1847 and 1857 — because it confused symptom with cause. **Hubbard's refutation** showed empirically that gold moved with the interest rate, not against the price level, falsifying the quantity-theory premise. The formal invariant: `MaxNotes = GoldBacking + UnbackedLimit`; any notes issued beyond MaxNotes under a suspension are recorded separately.

- **Domain types.** `NoteIssueRegime int` enum (1=`RegimePreAct1844`, 2=`RegimeBankAct1844`, 3=`RegimeSuspended`; `IsValid() bool`). New sentinels `ErrInvalidRegime`, `ErrNegativeGoldBacking`, `ErrNegativeUnbackedLimit`, `ErrNegativeNotesBeyondMax`. `NoteIssueConstraintID` + `NewNoteIssueConstraintID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `NoteIssueConstraint` (persisted entity; `GoldBacking`, `UnbackedLimit`, `NotesBeyondMax int64` pence ≥ 0; `MaxNotes int64` computed = GoldBacking + UnbackedLimit) via `NewNoteIssueConstraint(...)`. `CurrencyPrincipleAnalysis` pure-value type recording the Currency/Banking school debate and Hubbard's refutation. `GoldDrain` value type (`DrainAmount int64`; `TriggersSuspension bool` when drain exceeds Banking Department reserve).
- **Store.** Interface gains three methods (`CreateNoteIssueConstraint`/`GetNoteIssueConstraint`/`ListNoteIssueConstraints`). `memory.go` adds a `noteIssueConstraints` map with newest-first sort; `mysql.go` adds the three methods + `scanNoteIssueConstraint`. Migrations `00067_v3_ch34_note_issue_constraint.sql` (table `note_issue_constraints`) and `00068_v3_ch34_seed.sql` (seed IDs `5eed000000000000003400`–`3402`: Pre-Act flexible regime; Bank Act 1844 £14m fiduciary + gold backing; Suspension 1847 excess issuance).
- **HTTP endpoints (3).** `POST /v1/credit/note-issue-constraints` (400 bad JSON; 422 on invariant violations; 201 + Location), `GET /v1/credit/note-issue-constraints` (`items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/note-issue-constraints/{id}` (404 missing). New api-gateway proxy rules for the path prefix plus its `{rest...}` variant.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch34CurrencyPrinciple.tsx` (registered `v3-ch34`, status pending→done, circuitNode `["M","delta-M","historical"]`): explainer on the Currency School vs. Banking School, Peel's Bank Act splitting Issue and Banking departments, the 1847 and 1857 suspensions, and Hubbard's refutation (gold tracks interest rate, not prices); a note-issue-constraint form (name, regime select, gold backing, unbacked limit, notes beyond max, description) with a live computed Max Notes preview; stored-constraints table with regime badge (Pre-Act/Act-1844/Suspended), Gold Backing, Unbacked Limit, Max Notes, Notes Beyond Max, Description columns. `web/src/types.ts` adds `NoteIssueConstraint` and `CreateNoteIssueConstraintInput`; `api.ts` adds the three client functions (`createNoteIssueConstraint`, `listNoteIssueConstraints`, `getNoteIssueConstraint`). Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Ch. 35 — Precious Metal and Rate of Exchange

`finance-service` adds `internal/credit/gold_reserve.go` and `internal/credit/rate_of_exchange.go` (Vol. III Ch. 35, Part V). Gold functions as **world money** in its bullion form — the universal commodity that settles international balances where national credit instruments are not accepted. Every nation-state must maintain a **gold reserve fund** serving two roles: domestic last-resort reserve and the pivot of the international credit system. A falling reserve forces the central bank to raise its discount rate in order to attract gold inflows, demonstrating that the interest-rate instrument defends the gold reserve rather than expressing any intrinsic &ldquo;price of money.&rdquo; The **rate of exchange** measures deviation from mint parity — the ratio of gold contents in two national coins — and is determined by the **balance of payments** (including debt service, dividends, and capital remittances), not by the balance of trade alone. Gold drains to India and China in the 1850s illustrated a persistent payments deficit that coexisted with a favourable trade balance. The formal invariant: `DeviationBP` is a signed integer in basis points (positive = above par, gold flows in; negative = below par, gold drains out).

- **Domain types.** `WorldMoneyFunction` and `GoldFlowDirection` enums. New sentinels `ErrNegativeGoldReserveAmount`, `ErrInvalidGoldFlowDirection`, `ErrNegativeGoldFlowAmount`, `ErrInvalidWorldMoneyFunction`. `GoldReserveID` + `NewGoldReserveID()` and `RateOfExchangeID` + `NewRateOfExchangeID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `GoldReserve` (persisted entity; `Amount int64` pence ≥ 0; `Name`, `Description`) via `NewGoldReserve(...)`. `RateOfExchange` (persisted entity; `DeviationBP int64` signed; `Name`, `Description`) via `NewRateOfExchange(...)`. `BalanceOfPayments` and `GoldFlow` value types (pure, not persisted).
- **Store.** Interface gains six methods (`CreateGoldReserve`/`GetGoldReserve`/`ListGoldReserves`; `CreateRateOfExchange`/`GetRateOfExchange`/`ListRatesOfExchange`). `memory.go` adds two maps with newest-first sort; `mysql.go` adds six methods + two scan helpers. Migrations `00069_v3_ch35_precious_metal.sql` (tables `gold_reserves` + `rates_of_exchange`) and `00070_v3_ch35_seed.sql` (seed IDs `5eed000000000000003500`/`3501` for gold reserves: BoE Nov 1857 reserve and 1844 Act baseline; `5eed000000000000003510`/`3511` for rates of exchange: London–Hamburg 1847 −150 bp and London–India 1857 −200 bp below par).
- **HTTP endpoints (6).** `POST /v1/credit/gold-reserves` (201 + Location), `GET /v1/credit/gold-reserves` (`items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/gold-reserves/{id}` (404 missing); `POST /v1/credit/rates-of-exchange` (201 + Location), `GET /v1/credit/rates-of-exchange` (`items` never null), `GET /v1/credit/rates-of-exchange/{id}` (404 missing). New api-gateway proxy rules for both path prefixes plus their `{rest...}` variants.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch35PreciousMetal.tsx` (registered `v3-ch35`, status pending→done, circuitNode `["M"]`): intro on gold as world money, the BoE reserve as the pivot of credit, the rate of exchange tracking the balance of payments not trade, and the India/China gold drains of the 1850s; a Gold Reserve form (Name, Amount in pence, Description) calling `financeApi.createGoldReserve`; live table of stored reserves (Name, Amount via penceToPounds, Description, Recorded); a Rate of Exchange form (Name, Deviation from par in basis points — signed integer, Description) calling `financeApi.createRateOfExchange`; live table of rates (Name, deviation_bp shown with sign and colour — green above par / red below par, Description, Recorded); both lists fetched on mount and after create. `web/src/types.ts` adds `GoldReserve`, `CreateGoldReserveInput`, `RateOfExchange`, `CreateRateOfExchangeInput`; `api.ts` adds the six client functions. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`.

### Ch. 37 — Introduction (to Ground-Rent)

`finance-service` adds `internal/rent/` package (Vol. III Ch. 37, Part VI) — the **opening chapter of Part VI** (Transformation of Surplus-Profit into Ground-Rent). Ground-rent is surplus-value redirected to the **landowner** by virtue of the monopoly of land. The chapter introduces the **three classes** whose revenues correspond to the three great factors of production: the **landowner** (rent), the **agricultural capitalist** (profit), and the **wage-labourer** (wages). It also introduces the **three forms** of ground-rent: differential rent (arising from differences in soil fertility or location), absolute rent (arising from the monopoly of ownership itself), and monopoly rent (arising from pure scarcity above value). All three are deductions from surplus-value.

- **Domain types.** `RentForm int` enum (1=`RentFormDifferential`, 2=`RentFormAbsolute`, 3=`RentFormMonopoly`; `IsValid()` ⇒ 1..3). `FertilityGrade int` (1=A worst/regulating … 4=D best). New sentinels `ErrInvalidRentForm`, `ErrInvalidFertilityGrade`, `ErrNegativeRentAmount`, `ErrNegativePeriod`. `LandParcelID` + `NewLandParcelID()` and `GroundRentID` + `NewGroundRentID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `LandParcel` (persisted; `OwnerID`, `FertilityGrade`, `AreaHectares float64`, `Location`) via `NewLandParcel(...)`. `GroundRent` (persisted; `ParcelID`, `CapitalistID`, `LandOwnerID`, `Form RentForm`, `AmountLabourMinutes int64` ≥ 0, `PeriodYears int64` ≥ 1) via `NewGroundRent(...)`.
- **Store.** Interface gains nine methods (`CreateLandParcel`/`GetLandParcel`/`ListLandParcels`, `CreateLandowner`/`GetLandowner`, `CreateAgriculturalCapitalist`/`GetAgriculturalCapitalist`, `CreateGroundRent`/`ListGroundRents`). `memory.go` adds maps with newest-first sort; `mysql.go` adds the nine methods (the `form` column is backtick-quoted throughout). Migrations `00073_v3_ch37_rent_schema.sql` (tables `land_parcels`, `landowners`, `agricultural_capitalists`, `ground_rents`) and `00074_v3_ch37_rent_seed.sql` (seed IDs `5eed000000000000003700` parcel, `…3701` landowner Duke of Sutherland, `…3702` agricultural capitalist, `…3703` differential-rent record — Norfolk Grade-D parcel, the three-class seed fixture).
- **HTTP endpoints (5).** `POST /v1/rent/parcels`, `GET /v1/rent/parcels`, `GET /v1/rent/parcels/{id}`, `POST /v1/rent/rents`, `GET /v1/rent/rents`. List endpoints return `{"items":[...]}` (never null). New api-gateway proxy rules for the `/v1/rent/` prefix.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch37Introduction.tsx` (registered `v3-ch37`, status pending→done): explainer on Part VI — ground-rent as surplus-value redirected to the landowner, the three classes, and the three rent forms; a land-parcel form (owner ID, fertility grade A–D selector, area in hectares, location); a ground-rent form (parcel ID, capitalist ID, land owner ID, form selector, amount in labour-minutes, period in years); two live tables. `web/src/types.ts` adds `LandParcel`, `CreateLandParcelInput`, `GroundRent`, `CreateGroundRentInput`; `api.ts` adds five client functions. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx`. **OPENS Part VI** (Ch. 37–47) — ground-rent.

### Ch. 39 — First Form of Differential Rent (Differential Rent I)

`finance-service` extends `internal/rent/` (Vol. III Ch. 39, Part VI) — the **first concrete form of differential rent**, arising from the simultaneous cultivation of soils of **unequal fertility** with equal capitals. The market price is regulated by the worst soil; every better soil yields a surplus-product which landed property converts into differential rent. Differential rent I also arises from **location**: proximity to market reduces transport cost below the regulating price, yielding a rent equivalent to the saving.

- **Domain types.** `SoilGrade int` (1=A worst … 4=D best). `DifferentialRentIEntryID` + `NewDifferentialRentIEntryID()`, `DifferentialRentITableID` + `NewDifferentialRentITableID()`, `LocationRentFactorID` + `NewLocationRentFactorID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `DifferentialRentIEntry` (`TableID`, `Grade SoilGrade`, `CapitalAdvancedBP int64`, `OutputQuarters int64`, `PriceOfProductionBP int64`, `SurplusProductQtrs int64`, `MoneyRentBP int64`). `DifferentialRentITable` (`Description string`, `TotalRentalBP int64`, `RegulatingGrade SoilGrade`). `LocationRentFactor` (`ParcelID`, `TransportCostBP int64`, `RentEquivalentBP int64`). `ComputeDifferentialRentI(entries)`: regulating = entry with smallest output; for each entry `SurplusProductQtrs = OutputQuarters − RegulatingOutput`; `MoneyRentBP = roundHalfUp(SurplusProductQtrs × PriceOfProductionBP, 100)`; `TotalRentalBP = Σ MoneyRentBP`; `RegulatingGrade` = grade of regulating entry. Marx Table I: grades A–D, capital £250 each, outputs 100/200/300/400 qtr, reg. price £3/qtr → total rental £900.
- **Store.** Interface gains six methods (`CreateDifferentialRentITable`/`GetDifferentialRentITable`/`ListDifferentialRentITables`/`ListDifferentialRentIEntries`, `CreateLocationRentFactor`/`ListLocationRentFactors`). Entries persisted as `entries_json` TEXT column on `dr1_tables` (no separate entries table). Migrations `00077_v3_ch39_dr1_schema.sql` (tables `dr1_tables`, `location_rent_factors`) and `00078_v3_ch39_dr1_seed.sql` (seed ID `5eed000000000000003900` for the Marx Table I table row; entries `3901`–`3904` live inside `entries_json`).
- **HTTP endpoints (6).** `POST /v1/rent/dr1-tables` (returns `{table, entries}`), `GET /v1/rent/dr1-tables` (returns `{items:[table headers]}`), `GET /v1/rent/dr1-tables/{id}` (returns `{table, entries}`), `GET /v1/rent/dr1-tables/{id}/entries` (returns `{items:[entries]}`), `POST /v1/rent/location-rent-factors`, `GET /v1/rent/location-rent-factors` (returns `{items}`). New api-gateway proxy rules for `/v1/rent/dr1-tables` and `/v1/rent/location-rent-factors`.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch39DifferentialRentI.tsx` (registered `v3-ch39`, status pending→done): DR I intro (unequal fertility + location); (1) Build a DR I table form pre-filled with Marx Table I — description field + 4 output inputs (grades A–D); submit → `createDR1Table`; display returned table (total rental in £, regulating grade letter) and entries table (Soil | Capital(£) | Output(qtr) | Reg.Price(£/qtr) | Surplus-Product(qtr) | Money-Rent(£)); (2) Saved tables list; (3) Location rent factor form (parcel_id, transport_cost_bp, rent_equivalent_bp) with live list. `web/src/types.ts` adds `SoilGrade`, `DifferentialRentIEntry`, `DifferentialRentITable`, `DifferentialRentITableWithEntries`, `LocationRentFactor`, `CreateDifferentialRentIEntryInput`, `CreateDifferentialRentITableInput`, `CreateLocationRentFactorInput`. `api.ts` adds six `financeApi` methods. Wired into `ChapterShell.tsx` (CHAPTER_PANELS + QUOTES).

### Ch. 42 — Differential Rent II, Second Case (Falling Price of Production)

`finance-service` extends `internal/rent/` (Vol. III Ch. 42, Part VI) — DR&nbsp;II under a **falling price of production**. When successive capital investments on better soils raise output to the point where the worst soil (A) can no longer compete, it is driven out of cultivation and a better soil becomes the new regulating soil — causing the market price of production to fall. The chapter demonstrates the **compensation law**: even at a lower price per quarter, the grain-rent (in quarters) can double so that total money-rent is preserved or rises. Marx's Table&nbsp;IVd is the canonical fixture — soil&nbsp;A excluded, soil&nbsp;B the new regulator, price falls from 60s to 45s per quarter, grain-rent doubles, money-rent of £18 is unchanged. Additionally the **normal capital per acre** rises as cultivation intensifies (Marx: England moved from ~£8 to ~£12/acre between the Napoleonic wars and the mid-nineteenth century).

- **Domain types.** `FallingPriceVariant int` enum (1=Constant, 2=Decreasing, 3=Rising productivity). `SoilExclusionEventID` + `NewSoilExclusionEventID()`, `FallingPriceDR2OutcomeID` + `NewFallingPriceDR2OutcomeID()`, `NormalCapitalPerAcreID` + `NewNormalCapitalPerAcreID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `SoilExclusionEvent` (persisted; `ExcludedGrade int`, `NewRegulatingGrade int`, `NewPriceOfProductionBP int64`; `Validate`: `NewRegulatingGrade > ExcludedGrade`). `FallingPriceDR2Outcome` (persisted; `Variant FallingPriceVariant`, `ExclusionEventID`, `TotalMoneyRentalBP int64`, `TotalGrainRentQtrs int64`, `TotalOutputQuarters int64`, `TotalCapitalBP int64`). `NormalCapitalPerAcre` (persisted; `PeriodLabel string`, `CapitalPerAcreBP int64`). `ComputeFallingPriceDR2(investments []SuccessiveInvestment, newPriceBP int64) FallingPriceDR2Outcome` pure fn: `TotalGrainRentQtrs = Σ SurplusProfitBP`; `TotalMoneyRentalBP = roundHalfUp(TotalGrainRentQtrs × newPriceBP, 100)`; `TotalCapitalBP = Σ CapitalBP`; `TotalOutputQuarters = Σ OutputQuarters`. POST `dr2-falling-outcomes` loads the exclusion event (404 if missing).
- **Store.** Interface gains seven methods: `CreateSoilExclusionEvent`/`GetSoilExclusionEvent`/`ListSoilExclusionEvents`, `CreateFallingPriceDR2Outcome`/`ListFallingPriceDR2Outcomes`, `CreateNormalCapitalPerAcre`/`ListNormalCapitalPerAcre` (the Get is needed so the falling-outcome POST can load its referenced exclusion event, 404 on miss). `memory.go` adds maps with newest-first sort; `mysql.go` adds the seven methods. MySQL column for variant stored as `falling_variant` (avoids ambiguity). Migrations `00083_v3_ch42_dr2_falling_schema.sql` (tables `soil_exclusion_events`, `falling_price_dr2_outcomes`, `normal_capital_per_acre`) and `00084_v3_ch42_dr2_falling_seed.sql` (seed IDs `5eed000000000000004200` exclusion A→B at 540p and `5eed000000000000004205` exclusion A→B at 360p, `5eed000000000000004201` Table IV outcome £13.50 (grain 250 @ 540p), `5eed000000000000004202` Table IVd compensation outcome £18 (grain 500 @ 360p), `5eed000000000000004203` normal capital pre-1848 £8, `5eed000000000000004204` normal capital post-1848 £12; `-- +goose Down` DELETEs by those IDs).
- **HTTP endpoints (6).** `POST /v1/rent/exclusion-events` (201 + Location), `GET /v1/rent/exclusion-events` (`{items:[...]}` never null), `POST /v1/rent/dr2-falling-outcomes` (201 + Location; 404 if exclusion_event_id missing), `GET /v1/rent/dr2-falling-outcomes` (`{items:[...]}` never null), `POST /v1/rent/normal-capital` (201 + Location), `GET /v1/rent/normal-capital` (`{items:[...]}` never null). New api-gateway proxy rules for all three path prefixes.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch42DR2FallingPrice.tsx` (registered `v3-ch42`, status pending→done): intro on compensation law (grain-rent doubles, price halves, money-rent preserved) and rising normal capital per acre; (1) Soil-exclusion-events form — excluded_grade select A–D (1–4), new_regulating_grade select A–D, new_price_of_production_bp default 540; note "new regulating grade must be a higher grade than excluded"; list table (excluded→new regulating, price as £); (2) Falling-price DR&nbsp;II outcomes form — variant select (1/2/3), exclusion_event_id text field, two fixed investment tranche rows (parcel_id default `5eed000000000000003700`, capital_bp default 250, output_quarters default 400, surplus_profit_bp labelled "Grain-rent (1/100 qtr)" default 125); result table (variant, grain-rent in qtrs, money-rent as £, capital as £); (3) Normal capital per acre form (period_label default "England pre-1848", capital_per_acre_bp default 800); list table (period, £/acre). CSS prefix `v3-ch42`.

### Ch. 45 — Absolute Ground-Rent

`finance-service` extends `internal/rent/` (Vol. III Ch. 45, Part VI). Chapter 45 establishes that even the worst soil — which by definition yields no differential rent — always yields an absolute ground-rent. The source is agriculture's characteristically lower organic composition of capital (more variable relative to constant) than the social average, which causes agricultural products to sell near their value rather than at their (lower) price of production, generating surplus-value above the average profit. Landed property captures this excess by blocking capital from entering land without paying rent.

- **Domain types.** `AgriculturalCompositionID` + `NewAgriculturalCompositionID()`, `ValuePriceGapID` + `NewValuePriceGapID()`, `AbsoluteRentID` + `NewAbsoluteRentID()`, `AbsoluteRentLimitID` + `NewAbsoluteRentLimitID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `AgriculturalComposition` (persisted; `ConstantCapitalBP int64`, `VariableCapitalBP int64`, `CompositionRatioBP int64`, `SocialAverageRatioBP int64`, `IsBelowAverage bool`). `ValuePriceGap` (persisted; `ProductID string`, `ValueLabourMinutes int64`, `PriceOfProductionLabourMinutes int64`, `GapLabourMinutes int64`). `AbsoluteRent` (persisted; `ParcelID`, `ValuePriceGapID`, `SurplusValueBP int64`, `AverageProfitBP int64`, `AbsoluteRentBP int64`). `AbsoluteRentLimit` (persisted; `MaxRentBP int64`, `ActualRentBP int64`, `IsAtLimit bool`). Pure functions: `ComputeCompositionRatioBP(c, v int64) int64` = roundHalfUp(c×10000, v); `IsBelowAverage(ratio, socialAvg int64) bool` = ratio < socialAvg; `ComputeValuePriceGap(value, price int64) int64` = value − price; `ComputeAbsoluteRentBP(s, avgProfit int64) int64` = max(0, s − avgProfit); `IsAtLimit(actual, max int64) bool` = actual >= max. If agricultural composition rose to the social average the gap would close and absolute rent would vanish — only DR would remain.
- **Store.** Interface gains nine methods: `CreateAgriculturalComposition`/`ListAgriculturalCompositions`, `CreateValuePriceGap`/`ListValuePriceGaps`, `CreateAbsoluteRent`/`GetAbsoluteRent`/`ListAbsoluteRents`, `CreateAbsoluteRentLimit`/`ListAbsoluteRentLimits`. `GetAbsoluteRent` returns `ErrNotFound` on a missing ID (wired to HTTP 404). Migrations `00089_v3_ch45_absolute_rent_schema.sql` (tables `agricultural_compositions`, `value_price_gaps`, `absolute_rents`, `absolute_rent_limits`) and `00090_v3_ch45_absolute_rent_seed.sql` (seed IDs: `5eed000000000000004500` composition C=60 V=40 ratio=15000 social_avg=40000 is_below_average=true; `5eed000000000000004501` value-price gap product=agricultural-output value=140 price=120 gap=20; `5eed000000000000004502` absolute rent S=40 avg_profit=20 absolute_rent=20; `5eed000000000000004503` rent limit max=20 actual=20 is_at_limit=true; `-- +goose Down` DELETEs by those IDs).
- **HTTP endpoints (9).** `POST /v1/rent/compositions` (201 + Location), `GET /v1/rent/compositions` (`{items:[...]}` never null), `POST /v1/rent/value-gaps` (201 + Location), `GET /v1/rent/value-gaps` (`{items:[...]}` never null), `POST /v1/rent/absolute-rents` (201 + Location), `GET /v1/rent/absolute-rents` (`{items:[...]}` never null), `GET /v1/rent/absolute-rents/{id}` (200 or 404), `POST /v1/rent/rent-limits` (201 + Location), `GET /v1/rent/rent-limits` (`{items:[...]}` never null). New api-gateway proxy rules for all four path prefixes.
- **React panel.** `web/src/chapters/vol3/Ch45AbsoluteRent.tsx` (registered `v3-ch45`, status pending→done): intro on lower agricultural composition as source of absolute rent and value–price-of-production gap as its upper bound; (1) Agricultural compositions form — constant_capital_bp default 60, variable_capital_bp default 40, social_average_ratio_bp default 40000 → `createAgriculturalComposition`; list table (C, V, composition_ratio_bp, social_average_ratio_bp, is_below_average yes/no); (2) Value-price gaps form — product_id default "agricultural-output", value_labour_minutes default 140, price_of_production_labour_minutes default 120 → `createValuePriceGap`; list table (product, value, price, gap); (3) Absolute rents form — parcel_id default `5eed000000000000003700`, value_price_gap_id default `5eed000000000000004501`, surplus_value_bp default 40, average_profit_bp default 20 → `createAbsoluteRent`; list table (parcel, surplus_value, average_profit, absolute_rent); (4) Rent limits form — max_rent_bp default 20, actual_rent_bp default 20 → `createAbsoluteRentLimit`; list table (max, actual, is_at_limit yes/no). CSS prefix `v3-ch45`.

### Ch. 46 — Building Site Rent, Rent in Mining, Price of Land

`finance-service` extends `internal/rent/` (Vol. III Ch. 46, Part VI). Chapter 46 generalises the rent framework beyond agriculture to building sites, mines, and monopoly products, and derives the price of land as capitalised rent.

- **Domain types.** `LandPriceScenarioKind int` enum 1–5 (1=FallingInterestRate, 2=CapitalImprovements, 3=RentRisesWithProduct, 4=RentRisesMoreCapital, 5=RentRisesPriceFalls). `BuildingSiteRentID` + `NewBuildingSiteRentID()`, `MiningRentID` + `NewMiningRentID()`, `MonopolyPriceRentID` + `NewMonopolyPriceRentID()`, `LandPriceScenarioID` + `NewLandPriceScenarioID()` (96-bit crypto/rand hex). `BuildingSiteRent` (persisted; `ParcelID`, `LocationGrade int`, `AnnualRentLabourMinutes int64`, `LeaseTermYears int`). `MiningRent` (persisted; `ParcelID`, `OreGrade int`, `AnnualRentLabourMinutes int64`, `SurplusProfitBP int64`; grade-1 = regulating mine → zero rent). `MonopolyPriceRent` (persisted; `ParcelID`, `ProductKind string`, `ValueLabourMinutes int64`, `MonopolySalePriceLabourMinutes int64`, `MonopolyRentLabourMinutes int64`). `LandPriceScenario` (persisted; `Kind LandPriceScenarioKind`, `ParcelID`, `OldAnnualRentBP`, `NewAnnualRentBP`, `OldInterestRateBP`, `NewInterestRateBP`, `OldLandPriceBP`, `NewLandPriceBP int64`). Pure functions: `ComputeMonopolyRent(value, sale int64) int64` = max(0, sale−value); `CapitalisedLandPrice(rentBP, interestBP int64) int64` = roundHalfUp(rentBP×10000, interestBP).
- **Store.** Interface gains eight methods: `CreateBuildingSiteRent`/`ListBuildingSiteRents`, `CreateMiningRent`/`ListMiningRents`, `CreateMonopolyPriceRent`/`ListMonopolyPriceRents`, `CreateLandPriceScenario`/`ListLandPriceScenarios`. Migrations `00091_v3_ch46_land_price_schema.sql` (tables `building_site_rents`, `mining_rents`, `monopoly_price_rents`, `land_price_scenarios`) and `00092_v3_ch46_land_price_seed.sql` (seed IDs: `5eed000000000000004600` London West End building lease grade=5 rent=300 lease=99; `5eed000000000000004601` Scottish coal worst mine grade=1 rent=0 surplus=0; `5eed000000000000004602` Burgundy grand cru value=200 sale=400 rent=200; `5eed000000000000004603` land-price scenario kind=1 old_rent=10 new_rent=10 old_rate=500 new_rate=250 old_price=200 new_price=400; `-- +goose Down` DELETEs by those IDs).
